#### [1. Awesome Testing](/content/TheJambo/awesome-testing/README.md)

##### Software / AI & LLM Testing

* [OrcaReplay (⭐171)](https://github.com/Continuum-AI-Corp/OrcaReplay) ⭐ 247 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-09-15 - Records a coding-agent session below the harness, keeping the verbatim request and response bytes, then replays the same run offline with the network off or forks it from a checkpoint onto other models with a verify command deciding the verdict.
* [flight-recorder (⭐1)](https://github.com/xag/flight-recorder) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 7 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-09-11 - Record every nondeterministic input your code reads (LLM answers, HTTP, database, clock, randomness) as one JSONL tape per request, then replay the tape against the real code offline, bit for bit, with the first divergence named. Open format with Python, Node, .NET, Go, Java and PHP implementations.

##### Software / UI & End-to-End Testing

* [Drengr Runner (⭐1)](https://github.com/SharminSirajudeen/drengr-runner) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-08-31 - Drive an iOS simulator over HTTP from any language. A 479-line Swift XCTest target replacing WebDriverAgent on simulators. Apache-2.0.
* [Drengr](https://drengr.dev) - Mobile tests written as plain-English tasks instead of selectors, driven by an agent on real Android devices and iOS simulators. Works where there is no accessibility tree — canvas, games, Flutter without semantics labels — by falling back to vision and coordinates. Runs locally or in CI; bring your own model key.

* [Caveman.mcp (⭐1)](https://github.com/francescopaolopassaro/Caveman.mcp) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 C# | 📅 2026-08-13 - ![stars](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/francescopaolopassaro/Caveman.mcp?style=flat-square\&cacheSeconds=604800) ![last commit](https://img.shields.io/github/last-commit/francescopaolopassaro/Caveman.mcp?style=flat-square\&cacheSeconds=86400) An MCP (Model Context Protocol) server exposing Caveman's token compression, language detection, content-aware routing and extractive summarization as tools for AI agents and MCP-compatible editors — integrates directly with Claude Code, Cursor, Windsurf and similar.
* [Caveman.PrivacyGuard (⭐1)](https://github.com/francescopaolopassaro/Caveman.PrivacyGuard) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 C# | 📅 2026-08-17 - ![stars](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/francescopaolopassaro/Caveman.PrivacyGuard?style=flat-square\&cacheSeconds=604800) ![last commit](https://img.shields.io/github/last-commit/francescopaolopassaro/Caveman.PrivacyGuard?style=flat-square\&cacheSeconds=86400) 🛡️ Enterprise-grade PII/privacy analyzer for AI/LLM pipelines: detects, risk-scores and auto-masks sensitive data across 27 EU countries (heuristic regex + Luhn/IBAN/checksum validators + Shannon-entropy analysis), with GDPR/PCI-DSS/NIST-800-53 compliance mapping and runtime-configurable YAML rules. [NuGet](https://www.nuget.org/packages/Caveman.PrivacyGuard).

##### Editors

Individual developers get a free annual license. [Stilo.Editor NuGet](https://www.nuget.org/packages/Stilo.Editor) · [Stilo.Reports NuGet](https://www.nuget.org/packages/Stilo.Reports).

##### Tools / File

* [LibAFL (⭐2.6k)](https://github.com/AFLplusplus/LibAFL) ⭐ 2,643 | 🐛 120 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-09-07 (2022) - A Rust framework for building custom fuzzers from reusable components, with support for multiple platforms and scaling across cores and machines.
* [Driller (⭐981)](https://github.com/shellphish/driller) ⭐ 981 | 🐛 37 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2025-03-24 (2016) - A hybrid fuzzer that augments AFL with selective symbolic execution using angr to generate inputs for paths that fuzzing cannot reach.
* [QSYM (⭐654)](https://github.com/sslab-gatech/qsym) ⚠️ Archived (2018) - A concolic execution engine designed for hybrid fuzzing that works with AFL to generate inputs for new execution paths.
* [WINNIE (⭐576)](https://github.com/sslab-gatech/winnie) ⭐ 576 | 🐛 15 | 🌐 C | 📅 2022-11-28 (2021) - A Windows application fuzzer that combines harness synthesis with fast process cloning to test code beyond graphical interfaces.
* [AFLGo (⭐559)](https://github.com/aflgo/aflgo) ⭐ 559 | 🐛 22 | 🌐 C | 📅 2024-03-13 (2017) - An AFL-based directed greybox fuzzer that generates inputs to reach specified target locations in a program.
* [AFLFast (⭐424)](https://github.com/mboehme/aflfast) ⭐ 424 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 C | 📅 2023-06-16 (2016) - An AFL-based greybox fuzzer that uses power schedules to focus fuzzing effort on low-frequency execution paths.
* [REDQUEEN (⭐401)](https://github.com/RUB-SysSec/redqueen) ⭐ 401 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2021-09-23 (2019) - A binary fuzzer that uses input-to-state correspondence to overcome magic bytes and checksums without symbolic execution.
* [MOpt-AFL (⭐214)](https://github.com/puppet-meteor/MOpt-AFL) ⭐ 214 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 C | 📅 2021-01-26 (2019) - An AFL-based fuzzer that uses particle swarm optimization to adapt mutation operator selection probabilities for more effective fuzzing.
* [VUzzer (⭐183)](https://github.com/vusec/vuzzer64) ⭐ 183 | 🐛 14 | 🌐 C++ | 📅 2022-03-29 (2017) - An application-aware binary fuzzer that uses static analysis and dynamic taint analysis to guide input mutations, with a 64-bit implementation.
* [InsTrim (⭐84)](https://github.com/csienslab/instrim) ⭐ 84 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 C++ | 📅 2020-06-21 (2018) - A lightweight instrumentation approach for AFL that uses control-flow analysis to reduce the number of instrumented basic blocks while preserving execution path distinguishability.
* [G2FUZZ (⭐30)](https://github.com/G2FUZZ/G2FUZZ) ⭐ 30 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 C | 📅 2025-11-05 (2025) - An AFL++-based fuzzer that uses LLMs to synthesize and mutate input generators for grammar-aware fuzzing of non-textual formats.
* [AFL](https://lcamtuf.coredump.cx/afl/) (2014) - A coverage-guided fuzzer that uses instrumentation and genetic mutations to discover bugs. Its GitHub repository is archived; AFL++ is recommended for new projects.
* [Valgrind](https://valgrind.org) (2000) - A dynamic analysis framework with tools for detecting memory management and threading errors and profiling program execution.

* [CountDown (⭐29)](https://github.com/psu-security-universe/countdown) ⭐ 29 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2025-06-24 (2024) - A kernel fuzzer that uses shared reference counts to guide syscall sequences toward use-after-free bugs.
* [DR.FUZZ (⭐29)](https://github.com/secsysresearch/DRFuzz) ⭐ 29 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 C | 📅 2022-05-16 (2022) - A semantic-informed driver fuzzer that tests Linux device drivers without their hardware devices or device emulators.
* [Moneta (⭐27)](https://github.com/yonsei-sslab/moneta) ⭐ 27 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 C | 📅 2025-03-26 (2025) - A GPU driver fuzzer that recalls execution states captured on real hardware for ex-vivo fuzzing.
* [MOCK (⭐24)](https://github.com/m0ck1ng/mock) ⭐ 24 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2024-12-21 (2024) - A Linux kernel fuzzer that learns contextual dependencies between syscalls to generate context-aware test cases.
* [SyzTrust (⭐19)](https://github.com/SyzTrust/syztrust) ⭐ 19 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2024-02-29 (2024) - An on-device fuzzer for IoT trusted operating systems that uses state and branch coverage to guide input generation.

* [RGFuzz (⭐25)](https://github.com/kaist-hacking/RGFuzz) ⭐ 25 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-01-10 (2025) - A WebAssembly runtime fuzzer that uses compiler rules to guide test generation and compares execution results across runtimes and architectures.

* [FIRM-AFL (⭐463)](https://github.com/zyw-200/FirmAFL) ⭐ 463 | 🐛 45 | 🌐 C | 📅 2024-03-22 (2019) - An IoT firmware fuzzer that combines user-mode and system-mode emulation to improve fuzzing throughput for POSIX-compatible firmware.
* [Fuzzware (⭐385)](https://github.com/fuzzware-fuzzer/fuzzware) ⭐ 385 | 🐛 15 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-06-27 (2022) - A firmware fuzzer that models memory-mapped I/O accesses to test microcontroller firmware without full peripheral emulation.
* [Hoedur (⭐73)](https://github.com/fuzzware-fuzzer/hoedur) ⭐ 73 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2025-05-12 (2023) - An embedded firmware fuzzer that uses multiple input streams to represent interactions with different hardware interfaces.

##### Tools / Hypervisors & Virtual Devices

* [V-SHUTTLE (⭐100)](https://github.com/hustdebug/v-shuttle) ⭐ 100 | 🐛 12 | 🌐 C | 📅 2023-12-01 (2021) - An AFL-based fuzzer for virtual devices in hosted hypervisors, with semantics-aware input generation.
* [HyperPill (⭐65)](https://github.com/HexHive/HyperPill) ⭐ 65 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 C++ | 📅 2026-03-26 (2024) - A hypervisor fuzzer that uses the hardware virtualization interface to exercise hypervisor behavior without hypervisor-specific input grammars.
* [ViDeZZo (⭐40)](https://github.com/HexHive/ViDeZZo) ⭐ 40 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 C | 📅 2026-04-08 (2023) - A virtual device fuzzing framework that models dependencies within and between messages to test devices in QEMU and VirtualBox.

* [AHA-Fuzz (⭐18)](https://github.com/S2-Lab/AHA-fuzz) ⭐ 18 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2025-12-02 (2025) - An intent-aware greybox fuzzer that uses eBPF-based analysis to test hardened Android applications.
